+          (!preview||refs.timbers.some(t=>t.id===81&&t.geometry?.surface===1))&&await visible(card(1)),{preview,surfaces:surfaces.surfaces,refs});
+      }
+    }}
+  ];
+};
diff --git a/geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_cases_b.js b/geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_cases_b.js
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_cases_b.js
@@ -0,0 +1,240 @@
+/* C09-C16. Normal gestures stay trusted; explicit stale-node/state injections
+ * exercise the execution-time guard without substituting a production result. */
+'use strict';
+module.exports = s => {
+  const {send,read,key,check,pre,wait,visible,point,ctl,fill,card,act,search,frames,settle,
+    rawRight,right,rightCard,blank,mark,rows,events,commands,query,dump,checkpoint,noWrites,
+    unchanged,fixtureCommands,fixture,selectCard,setCamera,beginBusy,releaseBusy,disconnect,
+    online,focus,openParameter,settleParameterDraft,facePoint,hostCount,backToCanvas,objectLines,cardsVisible,
+    saveOld,invokeOld,history,same,expectNativeDialog}=s;
+  const selection=()=>read('JSON.stringify(oc.selection)');
+  async function exclusive() {check('ONE_MENU_HOST',await hostCount()<=1);}
+  async function geometryWithin(selector,viewport='#canvasViewport') {
+    const bounds=await read(`(() => {const e=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(selector)}),v=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(viewport)});
+      const r=e?.getBoundingClientRect(),p=v?.getBoundingClientRect();return r&&p?{visible:!!e.getClientRects().length&&!e.hidden,
+        r:{left:r.left,right:r.right,top:r.top,bottom:r.bottom,width:r.width,height:r.height},
+        p:{left:p.left,right:p.right,top:p.top,bottom:p.bottom},overflow:document.documentElement.scrollWidth-innerWidth}:null;})()`);
+    check('MENU_WITHIN_VIEWPORT',!!bounds&&bounds.visible&&bounds.r.width>0&&bounds.r.height>0&&
+      bounds.r.left>=bounds.p.left+7&&bounds.r.right<=bounds.p.right-7&&
+      bounds.r.top>=bounds.p.top+7&&bounds.r.bottom<=bounds.p.bottom-7&&bounds.overflow<=0,{selector,bounds});
+  }
+  async function faceFlow(width=1400,measure=false) {
+    await fixture('reject',width);const before=await checkpoint();
+    await rightCard(1);await exclusive();await ctl(act('open'));
+    await wait('FACE_WORKSPACE_READY',`!$('objectWorkspace').hidden&&oc.edit?.bid===1`);await settle();await exclusive();
+    await ctl('[data-face-tool="select"]');await ctl('#workspaceFitButton');await frames();
+    const p=await facePoint(4000,1500);
+    const hit=p&&await read(`document.elementFromPoint(${p.x},${p.y})?.closest('[data-face-hit]')?.dataset.faceHit`);
+    pre('REAL_FACE_EDGE_HIT',hit==='e:0:1',{p,hit});const m=await mark();await rawRight(p);
+    await wait('FACE_MENU_VISIBLE',`!$('feCtxMenu').hidden`);await exclusive();
+    check('FACE_MENU_TYPED_EDGE',await read(`fe.sel?.kind==='edge'&&$('canvasCtxMenu').hidden&&!!$('feCtxMenu').querySelector('[data-fe-act="op:notch"]')&&!$('feCtxMenu').querySelector('[data-oc-act]')`));
+    check('FACE_TRUSTED_CONTEXTMENU',(await events(m)).some(e=>e.type==='contextmenu'&&e.trusted));
+    if(measure)await geometryWithin('#feCtxMenu','#faceEditorViewport');
+    await ctl('#feCtxMenu [data-fe-act="op:notch"]');await wait('NOTCH_SIDE_FORM',`fe.op?.type==='notch'&&!$('feOpPanel').hidden`);await exclusive();
+    if(measure)await geometryWithin('#feOpPanel','#faceEditorViewport');
+    else check('FACE_MENU_RETAINED_WITH_FORM',await read(`!$('feCtxMenu').hidden`));
+    await key('Escape','Escape',27);await key('Escape','Escape',27);await frames();
+    check('FACE_ESC_CANCELS_ONLY_FORM_AND_MENU',await read(`$('feCtxMenu').hidden&&$('feOpPanel').hidden&&!fe.op&&fe.sel?.kind==='edge'`));
+    await backToCanvas();await rightCard(1);await exclusive();
+    check('NO_FACE_HOST_RESIDUE',await read(`$('feCtxMenu').hidden&&$('feOpPanel').hidden&&!$('feActionBar').classList.contains('fcm-suppressed')&&!$('faceEditorViewport').classList.contains('fcm-active')`));
+    await unchanged(before);
+  }
+  async function staleResult(before,variant) {
+    await invokeOld();const cmd=await commands(before.mark);
+    check('STALE_ACTION_ZERO_WRITES',cmd.every(c=>/^(?:dump|objects|surfaces|references|engineering-read|source-inspect|render|boundary \d+|obstacles \d+)$/.test(c)),{variant,commands:cmd});
+    check('STALE_ACTION_NO_NAV',await read('!oc.focus'),{variant});await unchanged(before);
+  }
+  async function retireVariant(variant) {
+    await fixture();
+    if(variant==='undo-redo')await fixtureCommands(['boundary 41 outline 0,0 1100,0 1100,800 0,800']);
+    await rightCard(41);await saveOld();
+    if(variant==='selection'||variant==='navigation-aba'){
+      await selectCard(42);if(variant==='navigation-aba')await selectCard(41);
+      pre('NAVIGATION_ADVANCED',await read('oc.navigationSequence>__p352Owner.navigation'),{variant});
+      if(variant==='navigation-aba')pre('SELECTION_ABA',await read(`oc.sel?.kind==='boundary'&&oc.sel.id===41`));
+    } else if(variant==='definition'){
+      await fixtureCommands(['boundary 41 outline 0,0 1100,0 1100,800 0,800']);
+      pre('DEFINITION_SAME_FRAME_NEW_REVISION',await read('ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame===__p352Owner.frame&&ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().revision>__p352Owner.revision'));
+      pre('REFRESH_AFTER_EDIT',await read('renderObjects().then(r=>r===true)'));
+    } else if(variant==='reincarnation'){
+      await fixtureCommands(['history-begin','delete boundary 41','boundary 41 outline 0,0 1000,0 1000,800 0,800','canvas-pos boundary 41 80 100','history-end']);
+      pre('SAME_ID_NEW_INCARNATION',await read(`ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame===__p352Owner.frame&&typeof ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame.ids.get(41)==='string'&&ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame.ids.get(41)!==__p352Owner.incarnation`));
+      pre('REFRESH_AFTER_RECREATE',await read('renderObjects().then(r=>r===true)'));
+    } else if(variant==='undo-redo'){
+      await focus('#canvasViewport');await key('z','KeyZ',90,2);await settle();
+      pre('UNDO_RETIRES_FRAME',await read('ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame!==__p352Owner.frame'));
+      await key('y','KeyY',89,2);await settle();
+      pre('REDO_RETIRES_OLD_FRAME',await read('ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame!==__p352Owner.frame'));
+    } else if(variant==='context'){
+      const r=await read(`apiRequest('reset')`);pre('RESET_OK',r.ok===true&&!r.preview,r);
+      await fixtureCommands(['boundary 41 outline 0,0 1000,0 1000,800 0,800','canvas-pos boundary 41 80 100']);
+      pre('REFRESH_AFTER_CONTEXT_CHANGE',await read('renderObjects().then(r=>r===true)'));
+      pre('CONTEXT_RETIRED',await read('ReferenceWorkspace.context()!==__p352Owner.context'));
+    } else if(variant==='connection'){
+      const session=await read('connection.session');
+      try {await disconnect();}finally{await online();}
+      await settle();pre('SAME_SERVER_SESSION',await read(`connection.session===${JSON.stringify(session)}`));
+    }
+    await settle();const before=await checkpoint();await staleResult(before,variant);
+  }
+  async function attemptBlocked(name,mode) {
+    await fixture();
+    if(name==='create')await right(await blank());else await rightCard(41);
+    const button=await point(act(name)),beforeFocus=await read('JSON.stringify(oc.focus)');
+    if(mode==='busy')await beginBusy();else await disconnect();
+    const m=await mark();
+    try {
+      // If the app retired the menu, that is an allowed rejection; never call a
+      // helper to resurrect it. If it remains, click its real current position.
+      const retained=await visible(act(name));
+      if(retained){
+        const p=await point(act(name));await s.click(p.x,p.y);await frames();
+        if(name==='create'&&await visible(act('new:btnNewSpace')))await ctl(act('new:btnNewSpace'));
+      } else console.log('P352_RETIRED_MENU '+JSON.stringify({name,mode,button}));
+      await noWrites(m,'BLOCKED_ACTION_ZERO_WRITES');
+      check('BLOCKED_ACTION_NO_NAV',await read(`JSON.stringify(oc.focus)===${JSON.stringify(beforeFocus)}`),{name,mode});
+      if(await visible('#canvasCtxMenu'))check('BLOCKED_REASON_READABLE',await read(`$('canvasCtxMenu').querySelector('.fcm-footer')?.classList.contains('has-reason')===true||!!$('canvasCtxMenu').querySelector('[aria-disabled="true"][title]')`),{name,mode});
+    } finally {if(mode==='busy')await releaseBusy();else await online();}
+    await settle();
+  }
+  return [
+    {id:'C09',run:async()=>{
+      await fixture();await selectCard(41);const before=await checkpoint();await rightCard(41);
+      pre('SEARCH_HAS_FOCUS',await read(`document.activeElement===document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(search)})`));
+      await key('Escape','Escape',27);await frames();
+      check('FIRST_ESC_CLOSES_MENU_AND_RESTORES_FOCUS',await read(`$('canvasCtxMenu').hidden&&document.activeElement===$('canvasViewport')&&oc.sel?.id===41&&oc.selection.length===1`));
+      await key('Escape','Escape',27);await settle();
+      check('SECOND_ESC_REACHES_WORKSPACE',await read('oc.selection.length===0&&oc.sel===null'));await unchanged(before);
+    }},
+    {id:'C10',run:async()=>{await faceFlow();}},
+    {id:'C11',run:async()=>{
+      // This variant is first so M3 cannot be hidden by outside-pointer close.
+      await fixture();await rightCard(41);
+      // Focus/hover while still current: their reason()->position() also checks
+      // current. The final true click must not accidentally test those guards.
+      await key('ArrowDown','ArrowDown',40);await key('Home','Home',36);
+      await key('ArrowDown','ArrowDown',40);await key('ArrowDown','ArrowDown',40);
+      pre('CURRENT_PROBE_DELETE_FOCUSED',await read(`document.activeElement?.dataset.ocAct==='delete'`));
+      const deletePoint=await point(act('delete'));
+      await send('Input.dispatchMouseEvent',{type:'mouseMoved',x:deletePoint.x,y:deletePoint.y,button:'none'});
+      await saveOld();const before=await checkpoint();
+      console.log('P352_FAULT C11 selection-only injection; same opening, connected node');
+      await read(`oc.selection=[{kind:'boundary',id:42}];oc.sel={kind:'boundary',id:42};void 0`);
+      pre('STALE_NODE_STILL_CONNECTED',await read(`__p352Old.isConnected&&CanvasContextMenu.open&&document.activeElement===__p352Old&&
+        __p352Old.contains(document.elementFromPoint(${deletePoint.x},${deletePoint.y}))`));
+      await s.click(deletePoint.x,deletePoint.y);await settle();
+      check('CURRENT_PROBE_TRUSTED_CLICK',(await events(before.mark)).some(e=>e.type==='pointerdown'&&e.trusted&&e.button===0));
+      await noWrites(before.mark,'STALE_ACTION_ZERO_WRITES');await unchanged(before);
+      check('ISOLATED_CURRENT_PRESERVES_BOTH',(await query('objects')).faces.some(f=>f.id===41)&&(await query('objects')).faces.some(f=>f.id===42));
+      if(s.currentOnly)return;
+      for(const variant of ['selection','navigation-aba','definition','reincarnation','undo-redo','context','connection'])await retireVariant(variant);
+    }},
+    {id:'C12',run:async()=>{
+      for(const target of [41,42]){
+        await fixture();await rightCard(41);await saveOld();const worldA=await dump();
+        await key('Escape','Escape',27);await rightCard(target);
+        pre('OLD_NODE_DETACHED',await read('!__p352Old.isConnected'));
+        pre('SAME_WORLD_BEFORE_REPLAY',(await dump())===worldA,{target});const before=await checkpoint();
+        await invokeOld();await noWrites(before.mark,'OLD_NODE_ZERO_WRITES');await unchanged(before);
+        check('OLD_NODE_NO_NAV',await read('!oc.focus'));
+        // The allowed stale response may close B: always open a new valid menu.
+        if(await visible('#canvasCtxMenu'))await key('Escape','Escape',27);
+        await rightCard(target);const fresh=await checkpoint();await ctl(act('delete'));await settle();
+        const cmd=await commands(fresh.mark),o=await query('objects');
+        check('FRESH_MENU_DELETE_COMMAND_ONCE',cmd.filter(c=>c==='line delete boundary '+target).length===1,cmd);
+        check('FRESH_MENU_NATIVE_DELETE',!o.faces.some(f=>f.kind==='boundary'&&f.id===target));
+        check('FRESH_MENU_ONE_HISTORY_STEP',await history.single(fresh.pin));
+        await focus('#canvasViewport');await key('z','KeyZ',90,2);await settle();
+        check('FRESH_DELETE_UNDO_RESTORES_WORLD',(await dump())===fresh.scene);
+      }
+    }},
+    {id:'C13',run:async()=>{
+      await fixture();await openParameter();await fill('#paramName','右鍵取消保留草稿');await settleParameterDraft();
+      const before=await checkpoint(),selectionBefore=await selection(),focusBefore=await read('JSON.stringify(oc.focus)');
+      const p=await point(card(41)),m=await mark();
+      await expectNativeDialog(()=>rawRight(p),{message:'參數有未套用的草稿。選擇確定會捨棄，取消可繼續編輯。',accept:false});
+      await settle();
+      check('DRAFT_CANCEL_TRUSTED_RIGHT_CLICK',(await events(m)).some(e=>e.type==='contextmenu'&&e.trusted&&e.card==='boundary:41'));
+      check('CANCEL_RETAINS_DRAFT_AND_NO_MENU',await read(`ParameterUI.pending&&$('paramName')?.value==='右鍵取消保留草稿'&&$('canvasCtxMenu').hidden&&!CanvasContextMenu.open`)&&
+        await selection()===selectionBefore&&await read('JSON.stringify(oc.focus)')===focusBefore);
+      await unchanged(before);
+    }},
+    {id:'C14',run:async()=>{
+      await fixture();const before=await checkpoint();await rightCard(42);await setCamera(-5000,-5000,.6);
+      await ctl(act('locate'));await settle();check('LOCATE_TARGET_VISIBLE',await cardsVisible([42]));await unchanged(before);
+      await rightCard(42);await ctl(act('props'));await settle();
+      check('PROPS_TYPED_TARGET_VISIBLE',await visible('#inspectorContent')&&await read(`oc.sel?.kind==='boundary'&&oc.sel.id===42&&$('inspectorContent').querySelector('.ib-title')?.textContent.includes('42')`));
+      await unchanged(before);
+      for(const mode of ['busy','offline'])for(const name of ['open','delete','create'])await attemptBlocked(name,mode);
+      // Positive nav control after recovery uses a fresh real menu.
+      await fixture();const recovered=await checkpoint();await rightCard(41);await ctl(act('open'));
+      await wait('RECOVERED_NAV_WORKS',`oc.focus?.t==='face'&&oc.edit?.bid===41&&!$('objectWorkspace').hidden`);await settle();await unchanged(recovered);
+    }},
+    {id:'C15',run:async()=>{
+      await fixture();const before=await checkpoint(),o=await query('objects');await right(await blank());await focus(search);
+      const selectionBefore=await selection();console.log('P352_LIMIT C15 synthetic composition contract; physical Chinese IME NOT VERIFIED');
+      await read(`(() => {const input=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(search)});
+        input.dispatchEvent(new CompositionEvent('compositionstart',{bubbles:true,data:''}));
+        input.dispatchEvent(new CompositionEvent('compositionupdate',{bubbles:true,data:'空'}));
+        for(const key of ['Enter','Escape','ArrowDown','ArrowUp'])for(const type of ['keydown','keyup'])
+          input.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent(type,{key,bubbles:true,cancelable:true,isComposing:true}));
+      })()`);
+      await frames();check('IME_KEYS_LEAVE_MENU_AND_FOCUS',await read(`!$('canvasCtxMenu').hidden&&document.activeElement===document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(search)})`));
+      await unchanged(before);
+      await read(`document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(search)}).dispatchEvent(new CompositionEvent('compositionend',{bubbles:true,data:'空'}));void 0`);
+      await ctl(search);await send('Input.insertText',{text:'選取這段文字'});await key('a','KeyA',65,2);
+      check('CTRL_A_ONLY_SELECTS_INPUT_TEXT',await read(`(() => {const n=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(search)});return document.activeElement===n&&n.selectionStart===0&&n.selectionEnd===n.value.length;})()`)&&await selection()===selectionBefore);
+      await send('Input.insertText',{text:'空間'});await frames();
+      check('UNIQUE_SPACE_SEARCH_RESULT',await read(`(() => {const n=[...$('canvasCtxMenu').querySelectorAll('.fcm-list [data-oc-act]')];return n.length===1&&n[0].dataset.ocAct==='new:btnNewSpace'&&n[0].getAttribute('aria-disabled')==='false';})()`));
+      const enter=await mark();await key('Enter','Enter',13);await settle();
+      const after=await query('objects'),ids=new Set(o.spaces.map(x=>x.id)),added=after.spaces.filter(x=>!ids.has(x.id)),cmd=await commands(enter);
+      check('IME_END_ONE_NATIVE_SPACE',added.length===1&&cmd.filter(c=>/^line space \d+$/.test(c)).length===1,{added,cmd});
+      check('IME_END_ONE_HISTORY_STEP',await history.single(before.pin));
+      const afterScene=await dump();check('IME_PRESERVES_EXISTING_OBJECTS',[41,42,43].every(id=>same(objectLines(before.scene,id),objectLines(afterScene,id))));
+
+      await fixture();await openParameter();await fill('#paramName','文字編輯右鍵');await settleParameterDraft();
+      const textBefore=await checkpoint(),controls=await read(`(() => {const root=$('parameterEditor');if(!root)return [];
+        return [...root.querySelectorAll('input:not([type="hidden"]),textarea,[contenteditable="true"],[contenteditable=""]')]
+          .filter(e=>!e.disabled&&e.getClientRects().length>0).map((e,i)=>{e.dataset.p352Text=String(i);return {selector:'[data-p352-text="'+i+'"]',tag:e.tagName,editable:e.isContentEditable,value:e.value??e.textContent};});})()`);
+      pre('REAL_TEXT_CONTROL_EXISTS',controls.length>0,controls);
+      console.log('P352_TEXT_CONTROLS '+JSON.stringify(controls.map(({tag,editable})=>({tag,editable}))));

diff --git a/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py b/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py
@@ -0,0 +1,492 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Run real CDP controls/mutants in temporary source snapshots (never edit repo).
+
+Requires native Linux (including a Linux Python inside WSL), with /proc.
+Windows process-tree cleanup is not supported and is rejected before launch.
+Each manifest names one existing CDP scope, its complete case IDs, and exact
+single-site source replacements. Native executables must already be built from
+the reported product revision. An assertion failure is a kill only after green
+controls and a completed harness result; startup/fixture/cleanup failures block.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import shlex
+import signal
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import time
+
+
+def digest(data):
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+
+
+def safe_path(root, relative):
+    if not isinstance(relative, str) or not relative or Path(relative).is_absolute():
+        raise ValueError('Expected relative repository path')
+    unresolved = root / relative
+    if unresolved.is_symlink():
+        raise ValueError('Snapshot does not follow symlinks: ' + relative)
+    path = unresolved.resolve()
+    if not path.is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
+        raise ValueError('Path escapes snapshot: ' + relative)
+    return path
+
+
+def command(argv, cwd, timeout=30, env=None):
+    return subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace',
+                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                          timeout=timeout, check=False)
+
+
+def proc_identity(pid):
+    """Read Linux process identity without treating a reused PID as our child."""
+    try:
+        raw = (Path('/proc') / str(pid) / 'stat').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
+        return None
+    fields = raw[raw.rfind(')') + 2:].split()
+    try:
+        actual_pid = int(raw.split(' ', 1)[0])
+        if actual_pid != int(pid):
+            raise ValueError('PID mismatch')
+        return {'pid': actual_pid, 'state': fields[0], 'group': int(fields[2]),
+                'session': int(fields[3]), 'start': int(fields[19])}
+    except (IndexError, ValueError) as error:
+        raise RuntimeError('Cannot verify Linux process identity: ' + str(pid)) from error
+
+
+def require_process_namespace():
+    if not sys.platform.startswith('linux') or not Path('/proc/self/stat').is_file():
+        raise RuntimeError('Requires native Linux/WSL Python with /proc; Windows owned-tree cleanup is unsupported')
+    # Some hosted executors expose a host /proc alongside virtual child PIDs.
+    # Never use that unrelated process table to decide what may be signalled.
+    self_stat = Path('/proc/self/stat').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    if int(self_stat.split(' ', 1)[0]) != os.getpid():
+        raise RuntimeError('/proc does not describe this process namespace; refusing to launch')
+    current = proc_identity(os.getpid())
+    if current is None or current['group'] != os.getpgrp() or current['session'] != os.getsid(0):
+        raise RuntimeError('/proc process-group/session identity disagrees with the OS; refusing to launch')
+
+
+def remember_owner(process):
+    # Popen(start_new_session=True) makes this child both group and session leader.
+    process.mutation_owner = proc_identity(process.pid)
+    owner = process.mutation_owner
+    if owner is not None and (owner['group'] != process.pid or owner['session'] != process.pid):
+        raise RuntimeError('Child did not acquire its isolated process group')
+
+
+def live_group_members(group):
+    members = []
+    for entry in Path('/proc').iterdir():
+        if not entry.name.isdigit():
+            continue
+        row = proc_identity(int(entry.name))
+        # A zombie holds no executable, socket or profile file; the parent reaps it.
+        if row is not None and row['group'] == group and row['state'] not in ['Z', 'X', 'x']:
+            members.append(row)
+    return members
+
+
+def stop(process):
+    if process is None:
+        return None
+    group = process.pid
+    owner = getattr(process, 'mutation_owner', None)
+    leader = proc_identity(group)
+    if owner is not None and leader is not None and leader['start'] != owner['start']:
+        raise RuntimeError('Owned process-group id was reused; refusing to signal it')
+    sent = []
+    for sig, grace in [(signal.SIGTERM, 5), (signal.SIGKILL, 5)]:
+        # Do not return just because the leader exited: its browser/bridge can live on.
+        if not live_group_members(group):
+            break
+        try:
+            os.killpg(group, sig)
+            sent.append(sig.name)
+        except ProcessLookupError:
+            pass
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + grace
+        while live_group_members(group) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
+            time.sleep(0.05)
+    remaining = live_group_members(group)
+    if remaining:
+        raise RuntimeError('Owned process group still has live members: ' + str(remaining))
+    process.wait(timeout=2)
+    return {'group': group, 'signals': sent, 'liveMembers': 0, 'completed': True}
+
+
+def snapshot(repo, destination, extras):
+    result = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(repo), 'ls-files', '-z'],
+                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+                            check=True)
+    names = set(os.fsdecode(x) for x in result.stdout.split(b'\0') if x)
+    names.update(extras)
+    files = {}
+    for name in sorted(names):
+        source = safe_path(repo, name)
+        if source.is_symlink():
+            raise ValueError('Snapshot does not follow symlinks: ' + name)
+        if not source.is_file():
+            if name in extras:
+                raise ValueError('Missing test input: ' + name)
+            continue  # A tracked deletion remains a deletion in the snapshot.
+        target = safe_path(destination, name)
+        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        shutil.copy2(source, target)
+        # Attest to the bytes actually executed, not a later read of a live worktree.
+        files[name] = digest(target.read_bytes())
+    return files
+
+
+RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV = frozenset([
+    'GEOMETRY_TEST_URL', 'GEOMETRY_FEEDBACK_DIR', 'GEOMETRY_CHROME',
+    'GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS', 'GEOMETRY_CHROME_PROFILE', 'GEOMETRY_CDP_PORT',
+    'GEOMETRY_TEST_SERVER_PATH_STYLE', 'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE', 'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_EXECUTABLE',
+    'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_WSL', 'GEOMETRY_MODEL_BASE', 'GEOMETRY_CDP_UPDATE_GOLDEN',
+    'GEOMETRY_CDP_FROM_P31', 'GEOMETRY_TEST_SCREENSHOT',
+    'GEOMETRY_GRID_SCREENSHOT_DIR', 'GEOMETRY_REPAIR_SCREENSHOT_DIR',
+])
+
+
+def subcase_environment(manifest, value):
+    allowed = manifest.get('subcaseVariables', [])
+    reserved = RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV | {manifest['scope'], manifest['caseFilter']}
+    if (not isinstance(value, dict) or not set(value).issubset(allowed) or set(value) & reserved or
+            any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item or '\0' in item for item in value.values())):
+        raise ValueError('Mutation env must use declared non-reserved subcase keys and string values')
+    return dict(value)
+
+
+def read_manifest(path):
+    value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+    required = ['schema', 'suite', 'scope', 'caseFilter', 'resultPrefix',
+                'cases', 'inputs', 'mutations']
+    if not isinstance(value, dict) or any(k not in value for k in required):
+        raise ValueError('Incomplete mutation manifest')
+    if value['schema'] != 'geometry-browser-mutations/1':
+        raise ValueError('Unknown mutation manifest schema')
+    for key in ['scope', 'caseFilter']:
+        name = value[key]
+        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_') or not name.replace('_', '').isalnum():
+            raise ValueError('Invalid environment key: ' + key)
+    if value['scope'] == value['caseFilter']:
+        raise ValueError('Scope and case filter must be distinct')
+    variables = value.get('subcaseVariables', [])
+    if (not isinstance(variables, list) or
+            any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_') or
+                not name.endswith('_ONLY') or not name.isascii() or not name.replace('_', '').isalnum()
+                for name in variables) or len(set(variables)) != len(variables) or
+            set(variables) & (RESERVED_GEOMETRY_ENV | {value['scope'], value['caseFilter']})):
+        raise ValueError('Invalid or reserved subcase variable declaration')
+    for key in ['cases', 'inputs']:
+        items = value[key]
+        if not isinstance(items, list) or not items or any(not isinstance(x, str) or not x for x in items) or len(set(items)) != len(items):
+            raise ValueError('Invalid or duplicate ' + key)
+    ids = set()
+    for mutation in value['mutations']:
+        if not isinstance(mutation, dict) or not {'id', 'case', 'assertions', 'changes'} <= mutation.keys():
+            raise ValueError('Incomplete mutation')
+        mid = mutation['id']
+        if not isinstance(mid, str) or not mid.replace('-', '').replace('_', '').isalnum() or mid in ids:
+            raise ValueError('Invalid/duplicate mutation id')
+        ids.add(mid)
+        if mutation['case'] not in value['cases'] or not mutation['assertions'] or not mutation['changes']:
+            raise ValueError('Mutation lacks a case, assertion or change')
+        subcase_environment(value, mutation.get('env', {}))
+        for change in mutation['changes']:
+            if not {'file', 'before', 'after'} <= change.keys() or not change['before'] or change['before'] == change['after']:
+                raise ValueError('Mutation must replace one nonempty exact site')
+    if not ids:
+        raise ValueError('No mutations requested')
+    return value
+
+
+def parse_result(log, manifest, expected_cases, exit_code, reject_transport=False):
+    cases, footer, malformed = [], [], []
+    for line in log.splitlines():
+        prefix = next((p for p in [manifest['resultPrefix'], 'CDP_RESULT '] if line.startswith(p)), None)
+        if prefix is None:
+            continue
+        try:
+            row = json.loads(line[len(prefix):])
+            if not isinstance(row, dict):
+                raise ValueError('Result is not an object')
+            (footer if prefix == 'CDP_RESULT ' else cases).append(row)
+        except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
+            malformed.append(str(error))
+    errors = []
+    assertions = []
+    ids = [x.get('case', x.get('id')) for x in cases]
+    valid_ids = all(isinstance(value, str) and value for value in ids)
+    if not valid_ids:
+        errors.append('Malformed case id')
+    final = footer[0] if len(footer) == 1 else {}
+    counters = ['passedAssertions', 'failedAssertions', 'uncaughtBrowserExceptions']
+    valid_counters = all(type(final.get(key)) is int and final[key] >= 0 for key in counters)
+    if len(footer) == 1 and (final.get('schema') != 'geometry-cdp-result/1' or not valid_counters):
+        errors.append('Malformed CDP footer schema or counters')
+    complete = (not malformed and valid_ids and len(ids) == len(expected_cases) and
+                set(ids) == set(expected_cases) and len(set(ids)) == len(ids) and
+                len(footer) == 1 and final.get('completed') is True and valid_counters and
+                final.get('uncaughtBrowserExceptions') == 0)
+    for case in cases:
+        status = case.get('status')
+        failures = case.get('failures', [])
+        if not isinstance(failures, list):
+            errors.append('Malformed failures list')
+            continue
+        if status not in ['passed', 'pass', 'failed', 'fail', 'error']:
+            errors.append('Unknown case status')
+        if status == 'error' or case.get('kind') in ['precondition', 'exception', 'cleanup']:
+            errors.append(case)
+        if status in ['passed', 'pass'] and (failures or case.get('kind') == 'assertion'):
+            errors.append('Passing case also reports an assertion failure')
+        if case.get('kind') == 'assertion' and status in ['failed', 'fail']:
+            name = case.get('assertion')
+            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
+                errors.append('Assertion failure has no valid name')
+            else:
+                assertions.append(name)
+        for failure in failures:
+            if (not isinstance(failure, dict) or failure.get('kind') != 'assertion' or
+                    not isinstance(failure.get('name'), str) or not failure['name']):
+                errors.append(failure)
+            else:
+                assertions.append(failure['name'])
+        if status in ['failed', 'fail'] and not failures and case.get('kind') != 'assertion':
+            errors.append('Failure without a named assertion')
+    if assertions and final.get('failedAssertions') == 0:
+        errors.append('Named failures contradict the zero-failure footer')
+    traces = {key: final.get(key) for key in ['firstTransportFailure', 'firstNetworkFailure']}
+    if reject_transport and len(footer) == 1:
+        if any(key not in final for key in traces):
+            errors.append('Single-case result is missing transport trace fields')
+        if any(value is not None for value in traces.values()):
+            errors.append('Unexpected transport/network failure in a mutation control or mutant')
+    green = (complete and not errors and not assertions and exit_code == 0 and
+             all(x.get('status') in ['passed', 'pass'] for x in cases) and
+             final.get('failedAssertions') == 0 and final.get('passedAssertions', 0) > 0)
+    return {'complete': complete, 'green': green, 'cases': cases,
+            'footer': footer, 'errors': errors + malformed,
+            'assertions': assertions, 'exitCode': exit_code, 'transportTraces': traces,
+            'transportPolicy': 'require-null' if reject_transport else 'suite-defined'}
+
+
+SAFE_CHROME_FLAGS = frozenset(['--no-sandbox', '--disable-setuid-sandbox',
+                             '--disable-dev-shm-usage', '--disable-gpu',
+                             '--disable-software-rasterizer'])
+
+
+def isolated_environment(environ):
+    # Only the executable and an explicit safe set of Linux flags may be inherited.
+    # In particular, no caller profile, port, output path, WSL bridge mode, model
+    # executable, fixture filter or golden update can override this run's values.
+    env = {name: value for name, value in environ.items() if not name.startswith('GEOMETRY_')}
+    if environ.get('GEOMETRY_CHROME'):
+        env['GEOMETRY_CHROME'] = environ['GEOMETRY_CHROME']
+    flags = shlex.split(environ.get('GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS', ''))
+    if any(flag not in SAFE_CHROME_FLAGS for flag in flags):
+        raise ValueError('GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS contains a flag outside the isolated-run allowlist')
+    if flags:
+        env['GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS'] = ' '.join(flags)
+    return env
+
+
+def run_cdp(root, manifest, options, output, label, case=None, extra_env=None):
+    run_dir = output / label
+    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
+    env = isolated_environment(os.environ)
+    with socket.socket() as sock:
+        sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
+        port = sock.getsockname()[1]
+        with socket.socket() as browser_sock:
+            browser_sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
+            browser_port = browser_sock.getsockname()[1]
+    env.update({manifest['scope']: '1',
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_URL': 'http://127.0.0.1:' + str(port) + '/',
+                'GEOMETRY_FEEDBACK_DIR': str(root.parent / ('feedback-' + label)),
+                'GEOMETRY_CHROME_PROFILE': str(root.parent / ('chrome-' + label)),
+                'GEOMETRY_CDP_PORT': str(browser_port),
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_SERVER_PATH_STYLE': 'native',
+                'GEOMETRY_BRIDGE_EXECUTABLE': str(options.bridge.resolve()),
+                'GEOMETRY_TEST_SCREENSHOT': str(run_dir / 'screens' / 'phase1-shell.png'),
+                'GEOMETRY_GRID_SCREENSHOT_DIR': str(run_dir / 'screens'),
+                'GEOMETRY_REPAIR_SCREENSHOT_DIR': str(run_dir / 'screens')})
+    if case is not None:
+        env[manifest['caseFilter']] = case
+    subcase_env = subcase_environment(manifest, {} if extra_env is None else extra_env)
+    if subcase_env and case is None:
+        raise ValueError('Subcase filters may not narrow the full-suite baseline')
+    env.update(subcase_env)
+    if options.model_base:
+        env['GEOMETRY_MODEL_BASE'] = str(options.model_base.resolve())
+    server = test = None
+    started = time.monotonic()
+    expected = [case] if case else manifest['cases']
+    result = {'complete': False, 'green': False, 'errors': [], 'assertions': []}
+    try:
+        with (run_dir / 'server.log').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as server_log:
+            server = subprocess.Popen([options.python, 'geometry-web/serve.py', str(port)],
+                                      cwd=root, env=env, stdout=server_log,
+                                      stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
+            remember_owner(server)
+            deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
+            probe = None
+            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+                if server.poll() is not None:
+                    raise RuntimeError('Isolated server exited: ' + str(server.returncode))
+                probe = command([options.python, 'geometry-web/runtime_version.py', '--probe', str(port)],
+                                root, timeout=10, env=env)
+                if probe.returncode == 0:
+                    break
+                time.sleep(0.25)
+            if probe is None or probe.returncode != 0:
+                raise RuntimeError('Runtime fingerprint/ready probe failed: ' + (probe.stdout if probe else 'no probe'))
+            (run_dir / 'runtime.log').write_text(probe.stdout, encoding='utf-8')
+            with (run_dir / 'cdp.log').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as test_log:
+                test = subprocess.Popen([options.node, 'geometry-web/cdp_test.js'],
+                                        cwd=root, env=env, stdout=test_log,
+                                        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
+                remember_owner(test)
+                exit_code = test.wait(timeout=options.timeout)
+            log = (run_dir / 'cdp.log').read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+            # Full-suite controls include intentionally offline cases. All current
+            # mutation cases are non-offline, so both their mutant and restored
+            # controls must have null transport traces. A future offline mutation
+            # needs an explicit expected/unexpected transport protocol first.
+            result = parse_result(log, manifest, expected, exit_code, reject_transport=case is not None)
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+        result['errors'].append(type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error))
+    finally:
+        result['cleanup'] = []
+        for process in [test, server]:
+            try:
+                cleanup = stop(process)
+                if cleanup is not None:
+                    result['cleanup'].append(cleanup)
+            except (OSError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+                result['errors'].append('cleanup: ' + str(error))
+                result['green'] = False
+        result['elapsedSeconds'] = round(time.monotonic() - started, 3)
+        result['command'] = [options.node, 'geometry-web/cdp_test.js']
+        result['scope'] = manifest['scope']
+        result['caseFilter'] = case
+        result['subcaseEnv'] = subcase_env
+        result['logs'] = str(run_dir)
+    return result
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument('--repo', type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
+    parser.add_argument('--manifest', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--model-base', type=Path)
+    parser.add_argument('--output-dir', type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument('--python', default=sys.executable)
+    parser.add_argument('--node', default='node')
+    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=900)
+    options = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        require_process_namespace()
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as error:
+        parser.error(str(error))
+    repo, output = options.repo.resolve(), options.output_dir.resolve()
+    if output.exists():
+        parser.error('--output-dir must be new; old receipts are never overwritten')
+    if output.is_relative_to(repo):
+        parser.error('--output-dir must be outside the product repository')
+    if not options.bridge.is_file() or (options.model_base and not options.model_base.is_file()):
+        parser.error('Pass existing native executable paths')
+    manifest_path = options.manifest.resolve()
+    manifest = read_manifest(manifest_path)
+    output.mkdir(parents=True)
+    report = {'schema': 'geometry-browser-mutation-receipt/1', 'suite': manifest['suite'],
+              'manifestHash': digest(manifest_path.read_bytes()), 'mutations': [],
+              'head': command(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], repo).stdout.strip(),
+              'bridgeHash': digest(options.bridge.read_bytes()), 'status': 'blocked'}
+    if options.model_base:
+        report['modelBaseHash'] = digest(options.model_base.read_bytes())
+    exit_code = 2
+    try:
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='geometry-browser-mutations-') as directory:
+            temporary = Path(directory)
+            baseline = temporary / 'baseline'
+            baseline.mkdir()
+            report['files'] = snapshot(repo, baseline, manifest['inputs'])
+            report['sourceHash'] = digest(json.dumps(report['files'], sort_keys=True).encode())
+            report['baseline'] = run_cdp(baseline, manifest, options, output, 'baseline')
+            if not report['baseline']['green']:
+                report['reason'] = 'Baseline is not green; no mutation counts as killed.'
+                return 2
+            for mutation in manifest['mutations']:
+                subcase_env = subcase_environment(manifest, mutation.get('env', {}))
+                row = {'id': mutation['id'], 'case': mutation['case'], 'status': 'blocked',
+                       'subcaseEnv': subcase_env}
+                report['mutations'].append(row)
+                work = temporary / mutation['id']
+                shutil.copytree(baseline, work)
+                originals = {}
+                try:
+                    for change in mutation['changes']:
+                        target = safe_path(work, change['file'])
+                        data = target.read_bytes()
+                        originals.setdefault(target, data)
+                        before, after = change['before'].encode(), change['after'].encode()
+                        if data.count(before) != 1:
+                            raise ValueError('Exact mutation site drifted: ' + change['file'])
+                        target.write_bytes(data.replace(before, after, 1))
+                        row.setdefault('changes', []).append({**change, 'beforeHash': digest(data),
+                                                            'afterHash': digest(target.read_bytes())})
+                    for target in originals:
+                        if target.suffix == '.js':
+                            syntax = command([options.node, '--check', str(target)], work)
+                            if syntax.returncode != 0:
+                                raise ValueError('Mutant syntax failure: ' + syntax.stdout)
+                    row['mutant'] = run_cdp(work, manifest, options, output, mutation['id'] + '-mutant', mutation['case'], extra_env=subcase_env)
+                except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+                    row['reason'] = str(error)
+                finally:
+                    for target, data in originals.items():
+                        target.write_bytes(data)
+                row['restored'] = run_cdp(work, manifest, options, output, mutation['id'] + '-restored', mutation['case'], extra_env=subcase_env)
+                mutant = row.get('mutant')
+                if not row['restored']['green']:
+                    row['reason'] = 'Restored control is not green.'
+                elif mutant and mutant['green']:
+                    row['status'] = 'survived'
+                elif mutant and mutant['complete'] and not mutant['errors'] and mutant.get('exitCode') == 1 and \
+                        mutant['footer'][0].get('failedAssertions', 0) > 0 and \
+                        set(mutant['assertions']).intersection(mutation['assertions']):
+                    row['status'] = 'killed'
+                elif 'reason' not in row:
+                    row['reason'] = 'No completed, named assertion failure; environment/fixture/timeouts do not kill.'
+            statuses = [x['status'] for x in report['mutations']]
+            report['status'] = 'passed' if all(x == 'killed' for x in statuses) else 'blocked' if 'blocked' in statuses else 'failed'
+            exit_code = 0 if report['status'] == 'passed' else 2 if report['status'] == 'blocked' else 1
+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
+        report['reason'] = type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error)
+        report['status'] = 'blocked'
+        exit_code = 2
+    except KeyboardInterrupt:
+        report['reason'] = 'Interrupted; incomplete cleanup or execution cannot pass.'
+        report['status'] = 'blocked'
+        exit_code = 2
+    finally:
+        (output / 'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'status': report['status'], 'receipt': str(output / 'receipt.json')}, ensure_ascii=False))
+    return exit_code
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    raise SystemExit(main())

此處指專案 App／瀏覽器／原生服務與真突變驗收；前述靜態語法及補丁套用檢查已實際完成。

1. 將三則 diff 區塊按順序串接成 `pr352-tests-v1.patch`，在上述基底的乾淨 checkout 先 `git apply --check`，再整套套用。回報實際 base、套用後 SHA／工作樹差異雜湊；不要把另一支 PR 的程式混入本收據。
2. 執行下列語法檢查，預期 exit 0：

```bash
node --check geometry-web/cdp_test.js
node --check geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_test.js
node --check geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_cases_a.js
node --check geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_cases_b.js
python3 -c 'from pathlib import Path; compile(Path("geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py").read_text(), "browser_mutation_check.py", "exec")'
```

3. 用該 checkout 的既有 build 流程產生 Linux bridge 與 model-base。下列絕對路徑換成此次實際編譯產物與 Linux Chrome；輸出目錄必須是尚不存在的新目錄。runner 自行建立隔離服務，不接使用者現用服務：

```bash

python3 geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py \
  --repo "$PWD" \
  --manifest geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_mutations.json \

  --model-base /absolute/path/to/this-head-linux-model-base \
  --output-dir /tmp/geometry-pr352-receipt-v1
```

正常測試是 `GEOMETRY_CANVAS_CONTEXT_MENU_ONLY=1`、C01–C16 共 16 案；runner 會在 baseline 通過後逐一跑突變。現 head 的已知缺陷若使 baseline 紅，預期整體 exit 2／blocked 並保留原始失敗，不算 killed。修正後應 baseline 全綠、每個指定突變 killed、每次 restored 全綠，最終 exit 0。突變仍綠為 survived／exit 1。請另核本機原生程序清理收據；本輪只做了 parser 與模擬 cleanup 的程式檢查。

4. 相關既有回歸與架構 gate：沿原隔離服務流程執行 `node geometry-web/cdp_test.js` 的 full 模式，並執行 `python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_product_architecture.py'`。先回報原始結果；新 P352 段的 golden 只能依真完整執行收據新增，不可預填數目、更新舊段門檻或改舊斷言。已知產品架構缺項要與本次測試登記分開報。

### 2026-10-27T18:56:36Z ZhiQiYang

【驗證回報】PR #352：瀏覽器測試 v1 實跑完成：16 案 baseline 未綠（734 過／10 敗），突變未執行

回應 ChatGPT 的測試 v1：
- [第 1 段](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/352#issuecomment-6437715796)
- [第 2 段](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/352#issuecomment-6437716561)
- [第 3 段](https://github.com/ZhiQiYang/geometry-axiom/pull/352#issuecomment-6437717291)

## 環境

前兩輪都是 0 個斷言、`no CDP`，屬於環境阻擋，不是測試或產品的結果。原因是 WSL 冷啟動後第一次開 Chrome 要約 29 秒才印出「DevTools listening」。探針實測：第一次 29.4 s，之後 0.6–0.9 s。`cdp_test.js` 只等 30 秒（120×250 ms）。

這一輪只改環境：

- 跑 runner 前先暖機 Chrome 兩次，和正式 CI 工具 `ci_run.py` 的做法相同。本輪暖機：108 s，然後 2 s。
- `--no-sandbox` 走 runner 白名單的 `GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS`。
- 測試、斷言、runner、manifest 一字未改。

執行內容：

- 乾淨 clone 固定 head `c8eae279efe1d14ddfdd654165c790522a577471`。
- `git apply --check` 與 `git apply` 套用 `pr352-tests-v1.patch`，均 exit 0。
- 所有改到的 `.js` 跑 `node --check`，`browser_mutation_check.py` 跑 `py_compile`，都 ok。
- Linux 編譯 `web_bridge` 與 `model-base`。
- 執行：`GEOMETRY_CHROME=/usr/bin/google-chrome GEOMETRY_CHROME_FLAGS=--no-sandbox python3 geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py --manifest geometry-web/canvas_context_menu_mutations.json …`
- 版本：Node v24、Chrome 153.0.8010.47、Ubuntu 24.04（WSL）。

## 結果

- runner exit 2，`status: blocked`，原因原文：`Baseline is not green; no mutation counts as killed.`
- baseline `complete: true`、exit 1，16 個案例都有執行，**734 個斷言通過、10 個失敗**。
- C01、C03、C07、C08、C09、C10 全過。
- 三個突變都**沒有執行**：baseline 不綠時，依 runner 規則不算任何突變被殺。

## 失敗清單（原文節錄）

| 案例 | 斷言 | 實際 | 我從程式能確認的原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| C02 | KEYBOARD_MENU_VISIBLE | 焦點在「進入面 41」（`g.card-enter`）時按 ContextMenu 鍵，等待逾時，`#canvasCtxMenu` 仍 hidden | 未確定。`vp` 上的 `contextmenu` 監聽有處理 `clientX/Y=0` 的鍵盤情況；因為等待先失敗，事件追蹤沒有記錄，無法分辨是沒收到 `contextmenu`，還是 `begin()` 回了 null |
| C04 | HOME_OLD_DOM_REMOVED | `false` | 舊的 `#homeButton` 仍在（`index.html:281`、`app.js:10043`）。第一片沒有做 GEO-219 減項 |
| C05 | HOME_REACHABLE_WHILE_WORLD_BLOCKED | `busy:true` 時右鍵後選單未出現 | `canvas-context-menu.js` 的 `begin()` 在 `oc.busy`（以及 `oc.tool!=='select'`、`oc.drawTool`）時直接回 null，整個選單都不開；測試要求檢視類動作（回原點）在忙碌、離線、矩形工具下仍可用 |
| C06 | NATIVE_CANVAS_POSITION_AT_CURRENT_TRANSFORM | 預期 `{x:1209,y:1166}`（右鍵點換算到目前視角），實際 `pos:[424,600]` | 實際值等於預期裡的 `centerX/centerY`，代表新建物件放在視窗中心。第一片的建立子選單只是代按既有建立鈕，沒有把右鍵位置傳下去 |
| C11 | STALE_ACTION_ZERO_WRITES（variant `definition`） | 失效後仍送出 `line history-begin`、`line delete boundary 41`、`line history-end` | `current()` 的戳記只比 on／focus／view3／context／frame／session／selection／sel／tool／draw，沒有包含目標物件本身的定義版本；物件定義改了，戳記仍判為現行 |
| C12 | OLD_NODE_ZERO_WRITES | 舊節點已脫離 DOM 後，仍送出同樣三筆寫入 | 同 C11 |
| C13 | CANCEL_RETAINS_DRAFT_AND_NO_MENU | `false` | 未確定。測試在參數草稿未套用時右鍵卡片，要求選單不開、草稿保留 |
| C14 | CONTROL_REACHABLE | `#canvasCtxMenu [data-oc-act="open"]` 中心點 `hit:false`（83×56，未停用） | 未確定。選單項目存在，但中心點被別的元素蓋住，或在可點範圍外 |
| C15 | FOCUS_TARGET_ACTIVE（前置） | `[data-p352-text="4"]` 無法取得焦點 | 這是前置條件失敗，不是產品斷言。可能是測試注入的文字控制項在目前 DOM 拿不到焦點，請測試端確認 |
| C16 | SUBMENU_BACK_TO_PARENT | `false` | 未確定。從建立子選單返回後，子選單仍在，或焦點不在「create」 |

另有兩筆前置錯誤記在 `errors`：C14 的 CONTROL_REACHABLE、C15 的 FOCUS_TARGET_ACTIVE。

## 判讀

- 這 10 項失敗大多對應第一片（c8eae279）**尚未實作**的規格：鍵盤入口、GEO-219 移除舊回原點鈕、忙碌時仍可回原點、在右鍵位置建立、物件級的失效判定、子選單返回。
- C15 是前置條件，可能是測試端的問題。

## 套用與 SHA-256

本串 2/5 至 4/5 是 P 的 3 段；5/5 是完整 T。只取各 `diff` 區塊內文，P 依段號直接串接，不插入空行；每段本身已有結尾 LF。不要將 P 各段分別 git apply（切點可能位於同一 hunk）。T 獨立存檔。

|---|---:|---|
| `pr352-implementation-P.patch` | 127775 | `33cf48dde5dd1205faf6c090f2373a3dcf652e8acd22519dd28ea800582ca311` |
| `pr352-implementation-T.patch` | 16387 | `6fc9ed259b98450c7a851fecc52572b940d11ba2c61244dc194626222654a5fa` |
| P 完整內容後直接接 T（不插字元） | 144162 | `dc7a98aa267e95c56134868e85879451a44fa179e8baf6d90653cd342224979b` |

tests-v1 仍是原三段串接，SHA-256 `8721dbcc6491a865b6efb7b621cb5aa391a121d580bb62ec07935ba9a037d426`。本輪沒有重送或替換它。

來源說明：本環境由固定 c8e 的 GitHub blobs 重建來源，逐一核 Git blob，再套經 SHA-256 確認的 tests-v1。乾淨套用檢查先反套 v1，核對全部被修改之既有檔案與 c8e blobs 相符，再重新套 v1／P／T。架構 inventory 另外用五棵完整 Git trees 核對，606 個 executable 檔沒有遺漏、額外檔或非預期改動。收據內 local HEAD 是重建快照，不是新的產品 commit；`finalProductHead` 保留 null。本機 Claude 依既定流程在分支最終 SHA 再驗證後推送。

## 五個待釐清案例：親自重現與根因

以下均已做隔離函式／DOM 層重現；Chrome 探針和完整原生 CDP 驗收分列，沒有把 VM mock 算成端到端通過。

| 測試案 | 判定與實際重現 | 修正 |
|---|---|---|
| **C02** | **v1 注入前置有誤，產品另補穩健入口。** 在 Chrome 156，`keyDown/keyUp` 產生 trusted key 但不產生 native contextmenu；`rawKeyDown/keyUp` 則產生 trusted contextmenu。用原 c8e adapter 跑 raw 版本也能開到原卡片，所以原失敗不能解讀成「產品完全沒有 ContextMenu 支援」。 | T 只對 ContextMenu／Shift+F10 改 rawKeyDown，保留原 `TRUSTED_KEYBOARD_CONTEXT` 斷言。P 保留瀏覽器預設事件，另補無 native synthesis 時的 keyup fallback；同一次只開一次，鍵盤只有顯示位置，沒有建立座標。 |
| **C13** | **產品 B3。** 函式 probe 讓 `selectObject` 回 false，原 begin 仍回另一 target、開單；草稿選取未成功也被當成功。 | begin／props 尊重 false；選取後核實實際單選 kind/id 與 target 相符，才建立 stamp。完整 C13 用真參數草稿與原生取消對話框驗證。 |
| **C14** | **產品 C1 的 CSS。** 原 `body.oc-busy button/input` 有 `pointer-events:none`。隔離 Chrome 用原 shell/CSS 實測，按鈕有尺寸，中心 elementFromPoint 仍 hit:false；加限於 canvas menu 的覆寫後 hit:true。 | 僅 `#canvasCtxMenu` 的 button/input 恢復 pointer-events；每項 nav/world 仍在執行前重查 guard。沒有把其它 busy 控制解鎖。 |
| **C15** | **測試前置錯誤。** 原產品函式建立的第 5 個欄位 `[data-p352-text="4"]` 是閉合 `#paramExchange` details 內的 `#paramSpecValue`。Chrome 中仍回 layout rect，卻不能取得焦點；真點 summary 展開後，同一欄位能取得焦點。 | T 在收集欄位前點原生 summary，新增 `TEXT_EXCHANGE_OPEN` 前置。全部原輸入欄位、焦點／IME／零命令斷言保留，不刪控件，不降低斷言。 |
| **C16** | **產品 C3 的 hover／鍵盤競態。** 原 shell 的 ArrowLeft 本身能關子層、聚焦父項；但 390px 左側兩角，子層移除後，靜止游標下的父項收到零位移 mouseenter，立刻重開。真 Chrome 原／修版、390／1280、四角共 16 組對照已重現；修版 8 組均關閉且焦點回父項，真 pointermove 後 hover 仍可重開。 | 鍵盤導航暫時持有 hover，直到真實 pointer movement 再交回；保留搜尋、翻側及原夾限斷言，子層限制高度並可滾動，沒有刪項。 |

本次修正的末輪審核另外重現 **Shift 先放、F10 後放** 的 pending 生命周期缺陷：初版修法在 keyup 又要求 Shift，會吞掉下一次右鍵。最終版 pending 保存觸發鍵、keyup 按該鍵清理；button 2 使用當次 pointer target，blur 清 pending。VM 原／修對照已親跑，T 的 C02 另加 trusted 放鍵順序、下一次右鍵 target 41、World/history 不變。產品舊版的 C02 前置問題與這個實作過程收口的問題分開記錄。

## P 的產品修正

- **B1**：stamp 加 navigationSequence、project id/contextToken/revision、native definition revision、history frame/group/undo top/redo top、typed target kind/id/存在性/定義，以及 boundary incarnation/loops。執行前重查；phase/session transition 會退休舊單，同 session 離線恢復也不能復活。panX/panY/zoom 不進 World stamp。
- **B2**：shell open／close 退休 openingGeneration；按鈕、search、composition、hover／子層閉包綁自己的 generation；run 核 generation、active、current、當期 action/reason、guard/prerequisite，保存 executingState，晚到錯誤也不污染後一個 opening。
- **B3**：拒絕離開草稿即不開另一物件選單；依賴選取的 props 同樣處理 false。
- **C1**：busy／offline／非 select 改為 action 守門，view 仍有入口；離線只開 ocv 祖先路徑，其他工具列／表單保留 inert，左鍵／拖曳／一般快捷鍵仍被 capture 阻擋。snap/grid 與工具列共用純檢視 handler，外點關單後不把事件交給底下寫入 handler。
- **C2**：保存 pointer 原始 client 錨點，動作執行時、關面板與 await 前，以當下 ocToCanvas 換算。多邊形／空間沿原流程傳 canvasPosition；參數只傳入本次 draftPos，沿既有確認才寫入；矩形仍啟動原工具，其餘六項仍開原候選／編輯器／對話框。沒有把卡片座標當幾何，也不宣稱十個入口所有後續產物都套同一位置。鍵盤無合法建立點時停用並說明先指定位置。
- **C3**：ContextMenu、Shift+F10、IME isComposing/keyCode229/composition 狀態、文字輸入/contenteditable 排除、Esc 焦點與子層返回均補齊。
- **C4(b)**：刪 index.html 的 homeButton 及 app.js listener，保留 ocHome。C04 的「舊按鈕不存在」負向斷言保留。BACKLOG 僅 K4／GEO-254 既有 GEO-219 那一條增加本次減項記錄。
- **C4(c)**：canvas adapter 登記 H，沿用 shell／face adapter 分類；更新變動之 executable test exclusion 的 hash。H source schema 不允許 sha256 欄位，其變動雜湊在 receipt.inputs；JSON 測試資產、CSS、BACKLOG 另有 hash。四份架構產物已重產，原 scanner／兩套裁判未修改。

head `5c5700b5c151390f8f5c22a74655ee446ecfd2f0`。WSL 依該 SHA 自己的 `integration-tests.yml` 跑 7 個 job，兩兩並行。

## 1. 結果

| job | 結果 | 摘要 |

## 2. canvas：P53-6 回歸（確定）

失敗的三條斷言：

```text
P53-6 malformed 留在可重試錯誤狀態 ✗
P53-6 network 留在可重試錯誤狀態 ✗
P53-6 bridge 留在可重試錯誤狀態 ✗
```

**斷言內容**（main 原有，`cdp_test.js`）：連線錯誤時要同時滿足
`!$('connectionRetry').hidden && !$('connectionRetry').disabled && $('app').inert`。

**對照組**：同一個 WSL 環境、同一個 runner，以修正前的 `c8eae279` 跑完整 canvas。
- 結果：exit 0，2,267 過／0 敗，completed=true，golden `matched`，P53-6 三條都 ✓。
- 本機其他 6 份完整 canvas 收據（包括 main＋#354 的 `b5b33ed1`）P53-6 也都通過。

**排除測試端**：
- 完整模式裡，新的 P352 套件在最後才執行（`cdp_test.js:7174`），晚於 P53，不可能污染 P53。
- T 對 `cdp_test.js` 的改動只有新增 scope 旗標與兩處呼叫 P352 套件，沒有碰 P53。

**原因**：P 在 `app.js` `showConnectionState()`（head 第 548–567 行）把

```js
for (const id of ["app", "objectWorkspace"]) $(id).inert = blocked;
```

改成

```js
const canvasView = connectionCanvasView();   // phase!=='ready' && known && oc.on && !oc.focus && !view3
$("app").inert = blocked && !canvasView;
$("objectWorkspace").inert = blocked;
```

連線錯誤時若停在物件畫布，`#app` 不再 inert，改為只把 `#ocv` 祖先鏈上的兄弟節點設成 inert。這是為了 C1「離線時檢視入口可用」，但直接違反了 P53-6 既有的「錯誤狀態下 `#app` inert」不變式。

**請在 P2 修正，P53-6 一字不改。** 例如：選單掛在 `#app` 之外，讓 `#app` 維持 inert；或用其他不改變 `#app.inert` 的方式讓檢視入口可達。改法由你決定，但 P53-6 與 C05 都要綠。

## 3. canvas 第二個症狀：P88 結尾中斷（根因未確認）

- P88 最後一步「建立一份空白新專案…」的原生對話框之後，測試 FATAL：`Browser evaluation failed: Error: Missing [data-component-tab="guide"]`。
- 當下 `FATAL_UI`：`connection.phase:"ready"`，但 `connection.error` 仍是「無法確認載入結果，已暫停編輯。請確認服務後重試。」；焦點在面 1，最後操作是進入面內工作區 →「構件」分頁。
- `c8eae279` 的對照組在同一段沒有中斷。
- 我沒有定位根因。它可能和上面同一段 inert／`connectionViewInert` 的保存與還原有關，也可能是錯誤狀態沒被清掉。請一併重現。

## 4. native 的一項失敗研判為偶發

- `py:test_local_launcher` 單獨重跑（rule 2）exit 0。
- 本機其他 6 份 native 收據中，這個 suite 都是 17/17 通過、耗時 4.6–6.9 秒；這次耗時 12.4 秒。

## P53-6 與離線 C05 接線

`#app`、`#objectWorkspace` 在 loading／error 時一律保持 `inert`。只有已知快取、停在物件畫布且沒有面焦點／3D 的狀態，才將**原本同一個 `#ocv` SVG** 暫放到 body 的唯讀容器；用 marker 保存原位置，恢復後放回，沒有複製資料或重綁原事件。

- 原 viewport 保持原位置與尺寸，選單夾限、畫布座標仍以它計算；唯讀容器跟隨 resize／scroll 更新。
- 右鍵與 ContextMenu／Shift+F10 走原選單 adapter。一般 pointer、click、drag、wheel、編輯快捷鍵仍在 capture 階段攔下。
- 右鍵 down／up 停止傳播但不取消瀏覽器預設，保留原生 contextmenu，同時不讓右鍵釋放完成斷線前未結束的拖曳／矩形手勢。
- 原 `writable`、`navigable`、選單執行前身分核對均保留；純檢視仍走原 home／fit／props 等處理器。
- 焦點依實際入口回到唯讀容器或原 viewport；恢復連線不搶走重試鈕焦點。
- 掛載時解除 `#app` 的整體 opacity stacking context，容器用 z-index 1，讓既有工具列、浮動右欄與選單留在其上。左右 sidebar 保持不透明外殼，只對內容作淡化，避免下方卡片透入屬性面板。這部分已作 CSS／DOM 結構複核，仍待真瀏覽器像素與命中驗證。

原跨兄弟節點的 `connectionViewInert` 快照移除，連線呈現不再保存／覆寫其他工作區自行管理的 inert 狀態。

## P88：修正可重現的測試等待缺口

原 `enterFocus()` 先發佈 `oc.edit`，之後 `openFaceWorkspace()` 才等待 `feLoadGuides()`，最後生成動態 guide tab。原 P88 末段只等 `oc.edit?.bid===1`，因此在延遲回覆時可先通過等待，再得到同文 `Missing [data-component-tab="guide"]`。

P2 只把該段**同一個既有 25 秒等待**改為：目前 face／boundary／context 正確、工作區已開、目前面的 inspector `dataset.bid` 正確且 guide tab 已生成。後面的 components → guide 真實點擊，以及捲動、命中、焦點與 World 斷言原文保留；沒有新增固定 sleep 或延長 deadline。

診斷直接抽取 checkout 裡的實際等待條件，執行原入口／導航函式並延遲 native 回覆：修前 exit 1、修後 exit 0；換面後舊回覆不得 render 的負向控制也通過。**這是 Node VM／明示 double 的重現，尚未重跑原 WSL 完整 canvas，不能宣稱已證明那次 FATAL 的唯一原因。** `ready` 仍留舊 `connection.error` 的行為在對照版也存在，本次沒有附帶改它。

## C01–C16 回歸範圍

以下為受影響分析，不是本輪 16 案瀏覽器通過聲明。

| 範圍 | 案例 | 要核對的行為 |
|---|---|---|
| 直接受接線／還原／樣式影響 | C02、C05、C09、C11、C14、C16 | 系統選單鍵與原卡片目標、離線 home 零寫入、Esc 焦點、連線代次淘汰舊選單、定位／右欄查看與手機疊層、夾限／尺寸／外點關閉 |
| 共用入口的回歸控制 | C01、C03、C04、C06、C07、C08、C12、C13、C15 | 卡片開啟、多選、純檢視、建立位置、刪除與拒絕、舊 DOM、草稿取消、IME／文字控制仍受既有守門 |
| 跨工作區隔離 | C10 | 面內選單與原 face host 的清理，不掛物件畫布的唯讀容器 |

`cdp_test.js`、`canvas_context_menu_test.js`、`canvas_context_menu_cases_a.js`、`canvas_context_menu_cases_b.js` 已對固定 head 的 blob 核對，**四檔逐 byte 未改**。P53-6、C05 與 C01–C16 的任何既有斷言都沒有放寬；P88 只改等待條件。

   `#connectionRetry` 重新讀取場景；不重送失敗操作。載入與錯誤期間主工作區
   使用 `inert`，文件快捷鍵也停用。未讀到場景的計數顯示 `—`，不顯示建立第一物件的空狀態。
   已讀到過則保留最後完整快取，明示可能過期。HTTP 403 不放寬來源驗證。
+- `#canvasOfflineView`：只有已知快取的物件畫布在離線時使用，位於 `#app` 外，
+  暫放原 `#ocv` 提供既有右鍵／系統選單鍵的純檢視動作；`#app` 與 `#objectWorkspace`
+  仍維持 `inert`。一般點擊、拖曳、滾輪及編輯快捷鍵不放行；右鍵保留原生選單事件，
+  不傳入一般畫布手勢處理器。恢復連線後同一 SVG 回原位置，不複製資料或重綁事件。
+  只讀容器依原 viewport 的尺寸與位置更新，浮動右欄保持在畫布上方。
 - `#faceDraftNotice`：重畫外環時在畫布上方占獨立列，不能遮住落點。
   `#faceDraftTitle` 顯示草稿頂點數，`#faceDraftMessage` 說明原輪廓、面積與返回保留。
   `#faceDraftFinish` 走既有閉合驗證；`#faceDraftCancel` 只清草稿，不刪面。
